- **2l-abandon-plan**: Mark the current plan as abandoned and rollback to the end of the previous plan. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-abandon-plan.md))

- **2l-commit-iteration**: Manually commit the current iteration even if validation failed or hasn't run yet. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-commit-iteration.md))
- **2l-continue**: Resume 2L autonomous development from where the previous session stopped. The session directly acts as the continuation orchestrator, detecting state and continuing orchestration. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-continue.md))
- **2l-dashboard**: Start a local HTTP server to view the 2L orchestration dashboard in your browser. The dashboard displays real-time events, active agents, and orchestration progress. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-dashboard.md))
- **2l-dashboard-stop**: Stop the dashboard HTTP server and free the port for other projects. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-dashboard-stop.md))
- **2l-explore**: Deploy explorer agents to analyze project requirements and gather intelligence for planning. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-explore.md))
- **2l-heal**: Fix validation failures with specialized healer agents. Max 2 healing iterations before escalation. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-heal.md))
- **2l-improve**: set -e ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-improve.md))
- **2l-list-iterations**: List all iterations across all plans with detailed status. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-list-iterations.md))
- **2l-list-plans**: List all plans with their status, iterations, and metadata. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-list-plans.md))
- **2l-mvp**: Execute complete 2L protocol for quick prototypes and MVPs with three access levels: full autonomy, vision control, or full control. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-mvp.md))
- **2l-next**: Manually advance to the next iteration in the current master plan. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-next.md))
- **2l-plan**: Create a master plan through interactive conversation. The session becomes the master planner, analyzing vision, running strategic exploration, and guiding you through iteration breakdown. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-plan.md))
- **2l-prod**: Execute complete 2L protocol with production-grade outputs: comprehensive tests, CI/CD pipelines, and security validation. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-prod.md))
- **2l-rollback**: Rollback the codebase to a specific iteration checkpoint using git tags. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-rollback.md))
- **2l-rollback-to-plan**: Rollback to the last completed iteration of a specific plan. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-rollback-to-plan.md))
- **2l-setup-db**: Interactive command to install PostgreSQL client (psql) and configure secure database access for 2L orchestration. This command will prompt for your sudo password, store it securely, and test connection to Supabase local database. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-setup-db.md))
- **2l-setup-mcps**: Guided setup for Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers that enhance 2L agent capabilities. - **2l-status**: Display the current state of all plans, iterations, and progress. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-status.md))
- **2l-task**: Execute a streamlined 2L workflow for quick tasks and isolated fixes. Skip exploration and planning for clear, focused work. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-task.md))
- **2l-validate**: Run comprehensive validation on integrated codebase to verify production readiness. ([Ahiya1/2L](https://github.com/Ahiya1/2L/blob/HEAD/commands/2l-validate.md))

- **_index**: Welcome to the Plakar commands reference. This section provides detailed documentation for all available Plakar commands, including usage, options, and examples.
